# SoccerTrainingApp — detailed delivery backlog

Updated 2026-09-08. Planning only. 165 source tasks preserve SP-001–SP-150 and add SP-151–SP-165 for IP-01–IP-05. 42 containers and 123 leaves are decomposed into 390 activities. No live tracker task or accepted implementation is created by this file.

Start with [Agent activity plan](soccer_agent_activity_plan.md), [master plan](soccer_delivery_master_plan.md) and [execution playbook](soccer_agent_execution_playbook.md). The JSON manifest generates this backlog and the three CSV variants.

## SP-162 — Implement private weekly goal and milestone state

Phase: P04 — Shared data, identity and offline foundation | Gate: G3 | Type: Task | Execution: Agent | Status: Backlog

Accountable owner: Technical lead. Reviewer: Backend / security reviewer. Suggested agent: Backend agent. Actual identities remain unassigned.
